no_change
Player: I study the manifest payment marks to trace who funded Captain Voss.
Narration: The ledger paper feels stiff and aged beneath your examination, bearing the distinct wax impressions of official seals. As you trace the ink lines of the payment marks associated with Captain Voss, a pattern begins to emerge in the cryptic notations. It appears these particular markings correspond not to standard guild disbursements, but to funding streams originating from a lesser-known merchant house operating near the Western Gate.
